- Verified combined percentage of that historical saving attributed to mechanisms: **not established**, not 0%.
- Remaining unexplained percentage: **not numerically established**; do not manufacture 100% minus an unestablished share.

| Mechanism | Measured raw-token difference per workflow when modified | Normal WL reduction versus that modified treatment | Verified share of the original Direct/WL saving |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

The C08/C25 percentages have their own modified-treatment denominators, not the historical Direct/WL gap. They can affect overlapping charges and **must not be added or presented as explained percentage points of the ~50% saving**. An inconclusive test is not proof of zero effect. Older descriptive gap normalizations are not verified causal shares.

A C08+C25 joint treatment could increase WL usage, but full automatic refresh plus 120-second wait-through-resumption is not established as Direct's historical behavior. Even a joint treatment that reproduces the original gap would not alone identify its historical cause. The prior statement that the pair could explain all ~50% is a possibility, not a supported prediction. If Direct costs100 and WL50, removing benefits would need to raise WL to100 (**+100% of WL**) to recover that entire gap; +50% of WL would reach only75.

2026-09-12 06:15 Rome — **Source-only host fidelity checks: core grouped-command FIFO matches; continuation text differs.** Both H and frozen Q2 execute RUN1, deliver/generate and queue its reply, execute RUN2 and separately queue its reply, finish the original group, then send any union edit ACK; queued operations do not run before the original group finishes. Current-group feedback defers DONE; a later accepted DONE leaves later generated replies unprocessed in both. Q2 additionally requires DONE last, queues then rejects empty replies, and acknowledges discard-only. H `render_patch_applied_prompt` directs DONE after focused PASS or reported external blocker and another edit only for a validation-found issue; Q2 `Host.consume` also explicitly allows concrete unresolved requirements, while both globally require the complete task. Further source differences include ordinary native checkout inspection, sequential rather than cross-agent ownership, fatal host command timeout, and the preserved Direct fix-template resupply. The initial three handoff tests stopped the provider while its final interruption was held; their teardown failures remain recorded. The first two work-unit fixtures allowed four responses when the scenario required five, so they failed despite provider exit 0. The corrected work-unit scenarios passed their plumbing checks, but all four work-unit captures retain the later observer-executable identity flag: that qualified PASS is not full accounting readiness. The separate trust diagnostic supplies final-observer verification. The first two read-inline diagnostics are my missing-primary-marker error; the corrected two are separate attempts, not overwritten predecessors.

## Evidence already established

The corrected subscription diagnostics verified exact delivered inputs and response-level accounting: the bundled-read input item was charged 116 input tokens per subsequent response, versus 8,514 for the full-inline item in that diagnostic. This demonstrates retained-input charging, not the net effect or the causal share of the historical savings. Generation is closed; the retained full-workflow read-factor analysis is parked under the candidate-first plan.

The implementation and frozen study inputs are committed. Required Rust checks passed (449 tests passed, eight ignored); the frozen-source study/gate test materialization passed 265 tests. Diagnostic failures and the original test-packaging failure remain recorded alongside their qualified follow-up results.

## Next steps

1. Retain the closed six-workflow outcome set, six unlaunched identities, original flags and the operator-induced configuration-monitoring gap. Do not restart the stopped supervisor or launch controls.
2. Use the comprehensive candidate map's first source pass to verify per-candidate evidence status across context, policy/work selection, workflow/provider lifecycle and accounting boundaries. Retain exact sources, WL/Direct differences, exclusions, isolated test boundaries and interactions; resolve remaining cohort-exposure questions. Do not treat the old short list as exhaustive.
3. Identify which mapped mechanisms actually reduce raw usage using saved evidence and, where necessary, separately recorded benchmark-only variants against the existing WL baseline. No new controls or Direct runs; preserve non-target behavior and all outcomes.
4. Quantify contributions only after mechanism identification, including interactions and the distinction between a marginal WL ablation effect and a share of the historical Direct–WL gap. Keep the parked read runs for this later work.
